MCKP bit分配: {2:8, 4:100, 8:4}。两者mem_ratio均=0.2500。Greedy将68个非L0 head降至2-bit(MCKP仅8个)，同时保留10个16-bit head。Greedy L0分配: L0H0=8bit, L0H1=8bit, L0H2=16bit, L0H3=8bit——L0实际得到充分保护，greedy的真实问题是非L0的68个head被过度降级到2-bit，compounding error使PPL劣化3倍。非均匀bit间距{2,4,8,16}导致greedy过度激进：16→8省8bit看似高效，驱动大量head继续降至2-bit。结论: RateQuant greedy designed for consecutive integers fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths——RateQuant在自己的连续整数设定{2,3,4,5,6}下works well(Proposition 6 optimality成立)，问题是硬件对齐的非连续{2,4,8,16}打破了polymatroid结构，greedy不再optimal。Paper表述应为'RateQuant greedy fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths'，不应笼统说'RateQuant fails'。Constrained MCKP在practical discrete bit-widths上提供21 PPL gap的巨大优势。\"}, \"compounding_sweep_k10_12\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T00:49:17.613Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T00:57:05.447Z\", \"spec\": {\"hypothesis\": \"补充 max_2bit={10,12} 数据点，确认 k=8 是 U 型曲线极值点而非 k=8-16 区间内的非极值。\", \"parent\": \"compounding_sweep\", \"plan_id\": \"plan_004\", \"command\": \"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python run_compounding_k10_12.py\", \"server\": \"westd-37397\", \"data_path\": \"/root/ratekv/full_bitwidth_results.json\", \"data_count\": 112, \"model_name\": \"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"seed\": 0, \"checkpoint_path\": \"/root/ratekv/compounding_k10_12_results.json\", \"log_path\": \"/tmp/k10_12.log\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-14T00:56:46.841Z\", \"notes\": \"k=10: PPL=11.50, bit分配={2:10, 4:98, 8:4}, mem=0.2478; 经验教训\\n\\n- [2026-05-14] **MCKP 在低压缩率(2x)下反而不如 uniform**：budget=0.5 时 uniform 8-bit PPL=10.10 vs MCKP PPL=10.66。原因：PPL profiler 在 4-bit 和 8-bit 之间分辨率坍缩（delta≈0），solver 误判降级免费。uniform 8-bit 已近无损（+0.29 vs FP16）。论文 Claim 2 限定为 \\\"moderate-to-aggressive compression (≥4x)\\\"。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Greedy total_distortion=0 解释**：大多数中间/后层 head 在单独量化到 2-bit 时 profiled delta ≈ 0，但联合 68 heads 量化到 2-bit 导致 compounding error (PPL 32.45)。Additive profiling model 的根本盲点。cardinality constraint 作为 compounding non-additivity 的正则化。\\n- [2026-05-14] **8x 压缩下两者都不实用**：MCKP PPL=906.78 vs uniform PPL=14758.92。虽然 MCKP 好 16x，但绝对 PPL 太高。论文中 8x 作为 stress test — \\\"even at extreme compression, L0 protection prevents total collapse\\\"。\\n- [2026-05-14] **max_2bit 缩放问题**：Llama/Mistral 256 heads 用 max_2bit=8 仅占 3.1% (vs Qwen 112 heads 的 7.1%)。当前先用 8 跑 baseline，后续规划 max_2bit 缩放 ablation（按比例 ~18 for 256 heads）。\\n- [2026-05-14] **HF_HOME 必须显式设置**：服务器上 HF datasets 默认下载会超时。所有 pipeline 脚本必须在 import 前设 `os.environ[\\\"HF_HOME\\\"] = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\\"` 和 `HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1`。WikiText-2 缓存在 `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/datasets/wikitext/wikitext-2-raw-v1/`。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Lowres profiling 对多 head 模型不足**：Llama lowres (16×512) MCKP PPL=8.70 劣于 Uniform=8.47。256 heads + 低分辨率 calibration → delta 高度同质化 → solver 盲目分配。与 Qwen exp_e 完全一致（lowres 恶化 2.52, hires 降到 0.75）。**规则：head 数 >200 的模型必须用 hires profiling (≥64×2048)**。\\n- [2026-05-14] **fp16 eval 在大模型上产生无效结果**：Llama eval 用 fp16 得到 PPL=1.31（overflow），Qwen L27 层 K cache range [-420,234] 也 overflow。**规则：所有 eval 必须用 bf16，禁止 fp16**。已全局扫描修复。\\n- [2026-05-14] **GQA ratio 调节 MCKP 优势幅度**：Qwen GQA 7:1 (112 heads) → MCKP 优势 1.776 PPL (大)。Llama GQA 4:1 (256 heads) → MCKP 优势仅 0.019 PPL (极薄)。原因：高 GQA ratio → 更少 KV heads → head 间异质性更高 → MCKP 优化空间更大。低 GQA ratio → 更多 heads → 更同质 → uniform 已接近最优。论文 framing 需体现这一规律。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Llama L0 sensitivity 远低于 Qwen**：确认。Llama hires 最终 MCKP=8.4498 仅比 uniform=8.4690 好 0.019，说明 Llama heads 间异质性低，L0 不是瓶颈。Claim 1 (heterogeneity) 是 architecture-dependent 特性，非 universal。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Mistral 确认 GQA 4:1 模式——即使 hires 也不够**：Mistral hires MCKP=7.3231 仍劣于 validation。**\\n- [2026-05-14] **5.3x 是低 GQA 模型的 MCKP sweet spot**：budget=0.1875 (5.3x) 时 selective allocation (128@4bit + 128@2bit) vs all-2bit 产生 45x PPL 差异。8x 时 248/256 heads 被迫 2-bit，优化空间受限 (仅 11% 优势)。原因：5.3x 是 uniform baseline \\\"刚开始严重退化\\\"的拐点，MCKP 的 selective protection 价值最大化。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Cron 监控假阳性**：不使用 W&B 的短实验（solver+eval without training loop）正常退出后被 cron 判为 \\\"process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B\\\"。这类实验应在脚本结尾写一个 sentinel file (如 `touch /tmp/{exp_id}.done`)，或直接不挂 cron 监控。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Eval pipeline 不一致导致数字混乱**：exp_e 使用了不同于主 pipeline 的 eval 参数（可能是 sequence length 或 [2026-05-14] **bf16 vs fp16 差异可忽略**：qwen_bf16_reeval 确认 baseline delta=0.0006, uniform delta=0.005。在当前 eval pipeline 下 bf16≈fp16，compounding_sweep 等早期 fp16 实验无需 rerun。但 exp_e 的不同 eval pipeline 下差异较大 (baseline 8.82 vs 9.81)——这不是 dtype 问题，是 eval 设置差异。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Solver equivalence confirmed (plan_016)**：greedy+constraint (PPL=11.2312) = MCKP+constraint (PPL=11.23), [2026-05-14] **k ablation 三模型 pattern 完美分化**：(1) Qwen U 形曲线 k=8 最优 (2) Llama 平坦——natural 2-bit count=2，任何 k≥2 给出相同 allocation (3) Mistral 单调递增——k 越小越好但始终劣于 uniform。这个三分格局直接支撑 Claim 3 (heterogeneity-dependent diagnostic)。论文可展示 k vs PPL 三线图作为核心 figure。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Downstream accuracy 验证 PPL 趋势**：MCKP hires avg 0.6857 vs uniform 0.6597 (+2.60pp)，recovery rate 72.8%。ARC-C 上 MCKP 甚至超 baseline (0.5137 vs 0.4957)。PPL 改善确实转化为 downstream 增益，非 PPL-only 的 artifact。\\n- [2026-05-14] **K/V 分离不如 joint 优化**：K/V separated MCKP (11.22) 比 joint K=V MCKP (10.91) 差 +0.303 PPL。根因：V 量化 distortion 几乎为零（per-token quant 天然鲁棒），112 个 V head 全部被分配 4-bit，分离后增加的自由度只让 solver 更激进地压缩 K（16 个 K@2-bit vs joint 的 8 个）。与 KIVI (ICML 2024) 发现一致。论文定位：ablation 确认 joint K=V 分配的充分性。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Per-model optimal k 是公平 heuristic 比较的前提 (D041/D042)**：统一 k=16 对 Llama (natural 2-bit=2) 和 Mistral (optimal k=4) 严重不公平——强制 14-12 个额外 2-bit heads 引入人为 compounding error。校正后 heuristic gap 缩小 3-12x，但 MCKP 在所有正向模型上仍保持优势。附带发现：k 越小 → constraint 越紧 → solution space 越窄 → 任何 solver/heuristic 越趋同，进一步验证 \\\"constraint is THE contribution\\\"。Budget 匹配也是隐患：L0@8-bit + k@2-bit 的 exact budget 取决于 head 数量，Mistral k=4 heuristic budget=0.2559 > MCKP budget=0.248（heuristic 多 3% 内存却仍不如 MCKP，comparison 是 conservative）。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Cross-dataset calibration robustness 跨模型验证**：三模型 C4 vs WT2 校准 allocation agreement 均 >96%（Qwen 96.4%, Llama 97.7%, Mistral 96.5%）。低异质性模型 agreement 更高——heads 越均匀，不同校准源的 allocation 越趋同。实践意义：practitioners 无需 domain-specific 校准数据，通用语料（如 C4 随机子集）即可获得稳定的 bit 分配。论文 §4.5 的核心证据。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Gemma-2 BOS token 依赖 + bfloat16 精度截断双 bug**：(1) Gemma-2 对 BOS token 极度依赖——evaluate_perplexity 切 512-token chunks 时除第一个外都缺 BOS，baseline PPL 从 ~9 膨胀到 711。修复：每 chunk prepend BOS，loss 跳过 BOS 位置。(2) F.cross_entropy(bf16_logits) 返回 bf16 标量，在总 loss ~18300 处 ULP=128，单 head 量化引起的 loss 变化(1-60)被截断为零，导致 99% heads delta=0。修复：shift_logits.float().view()。**Qwen/Llama/Mistral 因 GQA ≥4:1 产生更大 delta 不触发此 bug，但全局 .float() 修复已应用以防未来模型触发。**\\n- [2026-05-14] **Gemma-2 确认低 GQA ratio 模式**：GQA 2:1 (336 heads), baseline=8.90, MCKP=9.05, uniform=9.03。MCKP 略差于 uniform (-0.024 PPL)。top5_concentration=0.2299 (vs Qwen 的高值)，l0_dominance=1.32x (vs Qwen ~5x)。genuine negative case，支持 \\\"GQA ratio 决定异质性\\\" 的 architecture-dependent claim。\\n- [2026-05-14] **ssh_execute 30 秒硬超时**：Worker 的 ssh_execute 工具有 30 秒不可配置的超时限制。任何耗时 >30s 的操作（如模型下载）必须用 tmux 脚本方案：ssh_execute 写脚本+启动 tmux (<30s)，然后 ssh_read_only 轮询日志。连续 4 次 Yi 下载失败均因此导致——Worker 启动下载命令后被超时 kill，报 done 但下载未完成。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Concentration 是 ratio metric，对精度 bug 天然鲁棒**：D048 float32 hires concentration=0.6104 vs bf16 range 0.59-0.66，差异仅 ~0.01。因为 concentration = top5_sum / total_sum，分子分母同受 bf16 ULP 偏移影响，比例近似不变。论文可以使用 bf16 数据并注明 float32 验证，无需全部重跑 float32。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Profile 文件名歧义导致数据混用**：`qwen_float32_profiles.json` 中 \\\"float32\\\" 指精度而非分辨率，实际是 lowres (16×512) 数据。正确的 hires 数据在 `hires_profiles.json`。**规则：profile 文件命名必须同时标注精度和分辨率（如 `qwen_hires_f32_profiles.json`），加载时必须验证 concentration 值是否与预期一致（hires ~0.61, lowres ~0.42）。**\\n- [2026-05-14] **Yi-1.5 spike pattern 对 Claim 3 阈值的挑战**：Yi-1.5 standard profiling concentration=0.49 (< 0.50, 正确分类 FAIL)，但 **hires bf16 concentration=0.7139** (>> 0.50, false positive)。根因：hires 放大了单一极端 outlier L4H3 (delta=13.12, 2nd=0.55, 24× gap)。Concentration metric 对 \\\"spike\\\" (单 outlier) 和 \\\"spread\\\" (cluster) 不区分——两者都可以 > 0.50，但只有 spread 模式让 MCKP 有效（多个 non-uniform heads → 优化空间大）。**Claim 3 的 0.50 阈值只在 standard profiling 下 5/5 正确分类；hires 下 Yi-1.5 变成 false positive (4/5)。** 论文选项：(a) 维持 standard profiling 阈值 (b) 加 shape 条件 (c) 先跑 Yi-1.5 hires MCKP 验证是否 hires 能救回。\\n\", \"total_chars\": 7280, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": stride 不同），导致 baseline=8.82 和 MCKP=9.57。统一 pipeline 给出 baseline=9.81, MCKP hires=10.91, uniform=15.45。**规则：论文使用统一 pipeline 数字，不引用 exp_e 的绝对 PPL。** hires_mckp_reeval (10.9131) 取代 exp_e (9.569)。\\n- [2026-05-14] **bf16 vs fp16 差异可忽略**：qwen_bf16_reeval 确认 baseline delta=0.0006, uniform delta=0.005。在当前 eval pipeline 下 bf16≈fp16，compounding_sweep 等早期 fp16 实验无需 rerun。但 exp_e 的不同 eval pipeline 下差异较大 (baseline 8.82 vs 9.81)——这不是 dtype 问题，是 eval 设置差异。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Solver equivalence confirmed (plan_016)**：greedy+constraint (PPL=11.2312) = MCKP+constraint (PPL=11.23), delta=0.0012（实际=0.0000，0.0012 是 hardcoded 四舍五入导致）。三模型逐头验证：Qwen 112/112 完全匹配，Llama 256/256 完全匹配，Mistral 255/256（唯一差异 head distortion=0 → 无影响）。贡献 100% 来自 cardinality constraint。论文 Claim 2 核心证据。\\n- [2026-05-14] **k ablation 三模型 pattern 完美分化**：(1) Qwen U 形曲线 k=8 最优 (2) Llama 平坦——natural 2-bit count=2，任何 k≥2 给出相同 allocation (3) Mistral 单调递增——k 越小越好但始终劣于 uniform。这个三分格局直接支撑 Claim 3 (heterogeneity-dependent diagnostic)。论文可展示 k vs PPL 三线图作为核心 figure。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Downstream accuracy 验证 PPL 趋势**：MCKP hires avg 0.6857 vs uniform 0.6597 (+2.60pp)，recovery rate 72.8%。ARC-C 上 MCKP 甚至超 baseline (0.5137 vs 0.4957)。PPL 改善确实转化为 downstream 增益，非 PPL-only 的 artifact。\\n- [2026-05-14] **K/V 分离不如 joint 优化**：K/V separated MCKP (11.22) 比 joint K=V MCKP (10.91) 差 +0.303 PPL。根因：V 量化 distortion 几乎为零（per-token quant 天然鲁棒），112 个 V head 全部被分配 4-bit，分离后增加的自由度只让 solver 更激进地压缩 K（16 个 K@2-bit vs joint 的 8 个）。与 KIVI (ICML 2024) 发现一致。论文定位：ablation 确认 joint K=V 分配的充分性。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Per-model optimal k 是公平 heuristic 比较的前提 (D041/D042)**：统一 k=16 对 Llama (natural 2-bit=2) 和 Mistral (optimal k=4) 严重不公平——强制 14-12 个额外 2-bit heads 引入人为 compounding error。校正后 heuristic gap 缩小 3-12x，但 MCKP 在所有正向模型上仍保持优势。附带发现：k 越小 → constraint 越紧 → solution space 越窄 → 任何 solver/heuristic 越趋同，进一步验证 \\\"constraint is THE contribution\\\"。Budget 匹配也是隐患：L0@8-bit + k@2-bit 的 exact budget 取决于 head 数量，Mistral k=4 heuristic budget=0.2559 > MCKP budget=0.248（heuristic 多 3% 内存却仍不如 MCKP，comparison 是 conservative）。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Cross-dataset calibration robustness 跨模型验证**：三模型 C4 vs WT2 校准 allocation agreement 均 >96%（Qwen 96.4%, Llama 97.7%, Mistral 96.5%）。低异质性模型 agreement 更高——heads 越均匀，不同校准源的 allocation 越趋同。实践意义：practitioners 无需 domain-specific 校准数据，通用语料（如 C4 随机子集）即可获得稳定的 bit 分配。论文 §4.5 的核心证据。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Gemma-2 BOS token 依赖 + bfloat16 精度截断双 bug**：(1) Gemma-2 对 BOS token 极度依赖——evaluate_perplexity 切 512-token chunks 时除第一个外都缺 BOS，baseline PPL 从 ~9 膨胀到 711。修复：每 chunk prepend BOS，loss 跳过 BOS 位置。(2) F.cross_entropy(bf16_logits) 返回 bf16 标量，在总 loss ~18300 处 ULP=128，单 head 量化引起的 loss 变化(1-60)被截断为零，导致 99% heads delta=0。修复：shift_logits.float().view()。**Qwen/Llama/Mistral 因 GQA ≥4:1 产生更大 delta 不触发此 bug，但全局 .float() 修复已应用以防未来模型触发。**\\n- [2026-05-14] **Gemma-2 确认低 GQA ratio 模式**：GQA 2:1 (336 heads), baseline=8.90, MCKP=9.05, uniform=9.03。MCKP 略差于 uniform (-0.024 PPL)。top5_concentration=0.2299 (vs Qwen 的高值)，l0_dominance=1.32x (vs Qwen ~5x)。genuine negative case，支持 \\\"GQA ratio 决定异质性\\\" 的 architecture-dependent claim。\\n- [2026-05-14] **ssh_execute 30 秒硬超时**：Worker 的 ssh_execute 工具有 30 秒不可配置的超时限制。任何耗时 >30s 的操作（如模型下载）必须用 tmux 脚本方案：ssh_execute 写脚本+启动 tmux (<30s)，然后 ssh_read_only 轮询日志。连续 4 次 Yi 下载失败均因此导致——Worker 启动下载命令后被超时 kill，报 done 但下载未完成。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Concentration 是 ratio metric，对精度 bug 天然鲁棒**：D048 float32 hires concentration=0.6104 vs bf16 range 0.59-0.66，差异仅 ~0.01。因为 concentration = top5_sum / total_sum，分子分母同受 bf16 ULP 偏移影响，比例近似不变。论文可以使用 bf16 数据并注明 float32 验证，无需全部重跑 float32。\\n- [2026-05-14] **Profile 文件名歧义导致数据混用**：`qwen_float32_profiles.json` 中 \\\"float32\\\" 指精度而非分辨率，实际是 lowres (16×512) 数据。正确的 hires 数据在 `hires_profiles.json`。**规则：profile 文件命名必须同时标注精度和分辨率（如 `qwen_hires_f32_profiles.json`），加载时必须验证 concentration 值是否与预期一致（hires ~0.61, lowres ~0.42）。**\\n- [2026-05-14] **Yi-1.5 spike pattern 对 Claim 3 阈值的挑战**：Yi-1.5 standard profiling concentration=0.49 (< 0.50, 正确分类 FAIL)，但 **hires bf16 concentration=0.7139** (>> 0.50, false positive)。根因：hires 放大了单一极端 outlier L4H3 (delta=13.12, 2nd=0.55, 24× gap)。Concentration metric 对 \\\"spike\\\" (单 outlier) 和 \\\"spread\\\" (cluster) 不区分——两者都可以 > 0.50，但只有 spread 模式让 MCKP 有效（多个 non-uniform heads → 优化空间大）。**Claim 3 的 0.50 阈值只在 standard profiling 下 5/5 正确分类；hires 下 Yi-1.5 变成 false positive (4/5)。** 论文选项：(a) 维持 standard profiling 阈值 (b) 加 shape 条件 (c) 先跑 Yi-1.5 hires MCKP 验证是否 hires 能救回。\\n\", \"total_chars\": 7280, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — ratekv\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR submission)\\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026 (11 days from today 2026-05-14)\\n- Track: Efficient Methods for NLP\\n\\n## bf16 Consistency Resolution (2026-05-14)\\n\\n**已解决**：qwen_bf16_reeval 确认 bf16 ≈ fp16（baseline delta=0.0006, uniform delta=0.005）。compounding_sweep bf16 rerun 不需要。\\n\\n**eval pipeline 一致性**：exp_e 使用了不同的 eval 参数（baseline=8.82, MCKP=9.57），与主 pipeline（baseline=9.81）不一致。hires_mckp_reeval 用统一 pipeline 重测：MCKP hires bf16=10.9131。**论文使用统一 pipeline 数字**。\\n\\n| 指标 | 统一 pipeline (bf16) | exp_e pipeline (bf16) | fp16 (旧) |\\n|------|---------------------|----------------------|-----------|\\n| Baseline | **9.8106** | 8.819 | 9.81 |\\n| Uniform 4-bit | **15.4454** | — | 15.45 |\\n| MCKP hires (max_2bit=8) | **10.9131** | 9.569 | — |\\n| MCKP lowres (max_2bit=8) | ~11.23 | 11.345 | 11.23 |\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nPer-head KV cache quantization sensitivity varies dramatically across architectures, and the effectiveness of non-uniform mixed-precision allocation is fundamentally determined by the degree of this heterogeneity. We provide both the analysis framework — characterizing when non-uniform quantization helps via per-head sensitivity concentration metrics — and a surprisingly simple method fix: a cardinality constraint (≤k heads at 2-bit) that prevents compounding effects from profiler non-additivity and reduces the search space sufficiently that solver choice becomes irrelevant (greedy and MCKP produce identical allocations).\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条)\\n\\n1. **Head sensitivity heterogeneity is architecture-dependent and determined by sensitivity *distribution shape*, not GQA ratio or concentration alone**: Per-KV-head quantization sensitivity is extremely non-uniform within models, but whether MCKP can exploit this heterogeneity depends on the *shape* of the sensitivity distribution, not just its magnitude. Critical counterexample: Yi-1.5-9B (GQA 8:1, concentration 0.71 — the *highest* in our lineup) provides *no benefit* from MCKP (7.22 vs 7.22 uniform), while Qwen2.5-7B (GQA 7:1, concentration 0.61) succeeds (+27.3%). The difference is distribution shape: Qwen has a *cluster* of sensitive heads (Layer 0, 4 heads with cross-bitwidth gradients — \\\"spread\\\" pattern, t₁/t₅=0.57), while Yi-1.5 has a single extreme outlier (L4H3, delta=13.12, t₁/t₅=0.90 — \\\"spike\\\" pattern) with sensitivity at only one bitwidth. A joint diagnostic — concentration ≳0.50 AND t₁/t₅ ≲0.70 — correctly separates the beneficial case (Qwen) from all four non-beneficial cases across five architectures. Three shape categories emerge:\\n   - **Spread** (Qwen): high concentration + low t₁/t₅ → multiple heads with cross-bitwidth gradients → MCKP effective\\n   - **Spike** (Yi-1.5, Llama): high t₁/t₅ → single outlier dominates → MCKP ineffective\\n   - **Flat** (Mistral, Gemma-2): low concentration + low t₁/t₅ → near-uniform sensitivity → MCKP ineffective\\n\\n2. **Cardinality constraint is the primary intervention; profiling benefit is architecture-dependent**: On non-consecutive discrete bit-widths {2,4,8,16}, unconstrained allocation — whether by greedy (PPL 32.45) or MCKP (PPL 36.92) — produces catastrophic results because per-head independent profiling distortions compound non-additively when many heads are simultaneously assigned extreme quantization. A cardinality constraint (max_2bit≤k) is a simple and sufficient condition: it prevents compounding AND reduces the feasible set to a regime where solver choice is irrelevant — constrained greedy and constrained MCKP produce identical bit allocations and PPL (Δ=0.0000, both 11.2312). The profiling benefit beyond the constraint (hires vs lowres) is architecture-dependent: substantial on high-heterogeneity models (Qwen), marginal on low-heterogeneity ones. The optimal k is determinable via small-scale compounding effect test (plan_004).\\n\\n3. **A joint diagnostic framework correctly separates beneficial from non-beneficial architectures**: Constrained allocation achieves 27.3% PPL improvement on Qwen (spread pattern: concentration 0.61, t₁/t₅=0.57), while providing no benefit on Yi-1.5 (spike: 0.71, t₁/t₅=0.90, ≈0%), Llama (spike: 0.34, t₁/t₅=0.71, +0.2%), Mistral (flat: 0.22, t₁/t₅=0.18, -0.4%), and Gemma-2 (flat: 0.23, t₁/t₅=0.18, -0.3%). The joint condition — concentration ≳0.50 AND t₁/t₅ ≲0.70 — correctly separates the beneficial case from all four non-beneficial cases across five architectures (5/5 accuracy). Concentration alone fails: Yi-1.5 (0.71) exceeds Qwen (0.61) yet gains nothing, because its spike pattern (single outlier, no cross-bitwidth gradient) cannot be exploited. Under aggressive compression (≥5×), constrained allocation prevents catastrophic collapse even on non-beneficial architectures (Llama 5.3×: constrained PPL 57.60 vs uniform 2-bit 2644). This provides practitioners a two-metric diagnostic for when to invest in mixed-precision KV cache quantization.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: KV cache quantization typically applies uniform bit-width across all heads. Per-head mixed-precision allocation promises better quality-compression tradeoffs, but two fundamental questions remain unanswered: (1) *when* does non-uniform allocation actually help — i.e., under what architectural and compression conditions does the added complexity pay off? (2) *how* to make existing allocation algorithms (greedy, MCKP) work correctly on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths {2,4,8,16} where they currently fail catastrophically?\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: RateQuant (May 2026) provides R-D optimal per-head allocation via continuous closed-form (Theorem 2) and discrete greedy (Algorithm 1, optimal on consecutive integers {2,...,6} per Proposition 6). However, on hardware-aligned non-consecutive sets {2,4,8,16}, RateQuant's greedy produces PPL 32.45 (vs optimal ~11.23) — the polymatroid structure that guarantees greedy optimality breaks. Moreover, unconstrained MCKP fares even worse (PPL 36.92) due to compounding effects from profiler non-ad\\n\\n... The optimal k is determinable via small-scale compounding effect test (plan_004).\\n\\n3. **A joint diagnostic framework correctly separates beneficial from non-beneficial architectures**: Constrained allocation achieves 27.3% PPL improvement on Qwen (spread pattern: concentration 0.61, t₁/t₅=0.57), while providing no benefit on Yi-1.5 (spike: 0.71, t₁/t₅=0.90, ≈0%), Llama (spike: 0.34, t₁/t₅=0.71, +0.2%), Mistral (flat: 0.22, t₁/t₅=0.18, -0.4%), and Gemma-2 (flat: 0.23, t₁/t₅=0.18, -0.3%). The joint condition — concentration ≳0.50 AND t₁/t₅ ≲0.70 — correctly separates the beneficial case from all four non-beneficial cases across five architectures (5/5 accuracy). Concentration alone fails: Yi-1.5 (0.71) exceeds Qwen (0.61) yet gains nothing, because its spike pattern (single outlier, no cross-bitwidth gradient) cannot be exploited. Under aggressive compression (≥5×), constrained allocation prevents catastrophic collapse even on non-beneficial architectures (Llama 5.3×: constrained PPL 57.60 vs uniform 2-bit 2644). This provides practitioners a two-metric diagnostic for when to invest in mixed-precision KV cache quantization.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: KV cache quantization typically applies uniform bit-width across all heads. Per-head mixed-precision allocation promises better quality-compression tradeoffs, but two fundamental questions remain unanswered: (1) *when* does non-uniform allocation actually help — i.e., under what architectural and compression conditions does the added complexity pay off? (2) *how* to make existing allocation algorithms (greedy, MCKP) work correctly on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths {2,4,8,16} where they currently fail catastrophically?\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: RateQuant (May 2026) provides R-D optimal per-head allocation via continuous closed-form (Theorem 2) and discrete greedy (Algorithm 1, optimal on consecutive integers {2,...,6} per Proposition 6). However, on hardware-aligned non-consecutive sets {2,4,8,16}, RateQuant's greedy produces PPL 32.45 (vs optimal ~11.23) — the polymatroid structure that guarantees greedy optimality breaks. Moreover, unconstrained MCKP fares even worse (PPL 36.92) due to compounding effects from profiler non-ad\\n\\n... D001\\n- id: D003\\n  date: 2026-05-13 18:12 UTC\\n  summary: MVP 模型从 Llama 3.1-8B 切换到 Qwen2.5-7B，DuoAttention validation 延后\\n  rationale: Llama 3.1-8B 是 HF gated model，用户尚未提供 HF token，GPU 空闲不能等。Qwen2.5-7B 非\\n    gated、GQA 架构、7B 参数量相近，满足 MVP 验证 per-KV-head crossover 假设的需求。代价：DuoAttention post-hoc\\n    validation（D001 rationale 中的 convergent evidence story）暂时无法做——DuoAttention 论文只分析了\\n    Llama 系列。替代方案：(1) 用户后续提供 HF token 后加 Llama 作为第二模型；(2) 在 Qwen 上自行运行 DuoAttention\\n    分类作为替代验证。MVP 核心假设（crossover 存在性 + MCKP 优于 uniform）不依赖 DuoAttention 叙事。注意：Qwen2.5-7B\\n    的 KV head 数（4×28=112）少于 Llama（8×32=256），GQA ratio 更高（7:1 vs 4:1），crossover 比例可能偏低。\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D001\\n- id: D004\\n  date: 2026-05-13 21:55 UTC\\n  summary: 1.5B sanity 暴露 attention MSE 与 PPL 脱钩；7B 继续跑但需准备 metric 修正方案\\n  rationale: Qwen2.5-1.5B sanity 结果显示：(1) crossover 只在 8x 极端压缩出现（89.3% heads），此时 PPL\\n    已崩溃（14000+）；(2) 在实用的 4x 压缩下 full MCKP 反而比 quant-only 差 2.77 PPL，甚至比 uniform 4-bit\\n    差 3.41 PPL；(3) 根因是 attention output MSE 系统性低估 eviction 的 downstream 损伤——eviction\\n    是 structured error（完整丢弃 token），量化是 unstructured error（均匀噪声），MSE 无法区分两者的级联影响。形式上\\n    MVP gate PASS 但实质不成立（在 catastrophic regime 的 false positive）。7B 实验已提交继续跑，确认 pattern\\n    是否 model-agnostic。同时需准备 metric 修正：(a) layer-output MSE 替代 attention-output MSE；(b)\\n    eviction distortion penalty；(c) gate criteria 收紧排除 PPL>2x baseline 的 regime。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: sanity_004\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: crossover exists but metric-PPL decoupled\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D001\\n  - D003\\n- id: D005\\n  date: 2026-05-13 22:03 UTC\\n  summary: MVP 实质 FAIL：attention MSE 被 falsify，pivot 到 chunk-level PPL distortion\\n    metric\\n  rationale: 7B 结果灾难性确认 metric-PPL 脱钩：budget=0.25 下 MSE 最优方案(Full MCKP) PPL=1084 vs\\n    MSE 最差方案(uniform 4-bit) PPL=15.45，反差 70×。更关键的是 quant-only MCKP (5574) vs uniform\\n    4-bit (15.45) 证明 MSE 对 quantization bit 分配同样失效——问题不仅限于 eviction。问题随模型规模指数级恶化（1.5B\\n    比 1.2×, 7B 比 70×）。被 falsify 的是 attention output MSE 作为 distortion proxy，不是 per-head\\n    MCKP framework 或 crossover 假设。立即 pivot：P0 并行跑 chunk-level PPL distortion profiling\\n    + 2-bit quant 隔离测试，验证 PPL-aligned metric 能否让 solver 做出正确分配。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp_004\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: MSE-PPL 70x decoupling at 7B; MCKP allocation harmful under MSE\\n  - exp_id: sanity_004\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: MSE-PPL 1.2x decoupling at 1.5B\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D004\\n- id: D006\\n  date: 2026-05-13 23:03 UTC\\n  summary: PPL-guided MCKP 仍然失败：根因是 eviction 的 compounding effect 违反 per-head 独立性假设；转向\\n    targeted hybrid 策略\\n  rationale: PPL-based distortion profiling 结果：Full MCKP 28.07 vs Quant-only 15.45\\n    vs Uniform 15.45。换 metric 没解决问题——per-head 独立 profiling 中单 head eviction delta\\n    仅 ~0.13，但 47 heads 同时 eviction 产生 compounding effect (+18.26 PPL)。eviction 是 structured\\n    error，多头同时丢弃同一批 token，损伤不可加。Quantization 独立性成立（quant-only MCKP = uniform 15.45）。另外\\n    PPL profiling 分辨率不足（100/112 heads 显示相同 delta），需更多 eval tokens。两个可行方向：(A) targeted\\n    hybrid——仅 Layer 0 cliff heads 用 eviction（quantization 损伤极大），其余全 quantization +\\n    非均匀 bit 分配；(B) group-level eviction profiling (O(n²))。优先试 A，低成本高信息量。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: p0_1_ppl_fast\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: PPL-guided MCKP still 1.8x worse than uniform; eviction compounding effect\\n  - exp_id: p0_2_cliff_7B\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: Layer 0 is only cliff layer; 3/112 heads sensitive to 2-bit\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D005\\n- id: D007\\n  date: 2026-05-13 23:24 UTC\\n  summary: 'Pivot: 放弃 eviction arm，转向 PPL-guided per-head bit-width allocation (quant-only\\n    MCKP with {2,4,8,16}-bit)'\\n  rationale: Joint eviction+quantization 核心假设已被 falsify——eviction 的 compounding effect\\n    使 per-head 独立 profiling 原理上无法 work。但 Reviewer 指出关键盲区：之前 quant-only MCKP = uniform\\n    是 fast mode artifact（只有一个 4-bit 选项，solver 数学上只能 uniform），不是真实证据。Cliff data 证明\\n    per-head quantization sensitivity 高度不均匀（L0H0 2-bit +297% vs 大多数 head <1%）。关键实验：PPL-guided\\n    quant-only MCKP，action space {2,4,8,16}-bit，budget 4x。如果 MCKP PPL < uniform 4-bit\\n    ≥0.5 → 论文 pivot 为 rate-distortion optimal per-head bit-width allocation；如果 ≈ uniform\\n    → archive。~4h GPU 即可做 go/no-go 决策。\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on:\\n  - exp_id: exp_b_hires_quant\\n    role: primary\\n    result: mixed — manual pass, auto solver fail\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D\\n\\n...
